= SAP Bibliography
:description: Every source cited in the SAP section, grouped as General, Finance and Human Resources, with the books, a list of non-official and sign-in sources, and the date the links were checked.
:keywords: SAP bibliography, SAP Help Portal, SAP S/4HANA, SAP BTP, CAP, SAPUI5, SAP Learning, SAP PRESS, SAP Finance sources, SAP HR sources

include::partial$ai-disclaimer.adoc[]

This page lists the sources that the pages of the xref:erp/sap/index.adoc[SAP section] cite, so that you can find the original text behind a statement. A source that only Finance pages cite is in the Finance group, one that only Human Resources pages cite is in the Human Resources group, and everything else is in the General group. Each entry names the source and one page that cites it.

Checked on 2026-11-13: 1729 addresses were requested without signing in. SAP Help Portal topics were checked against the portal's own topic service, because the portal pages load their text with JavaScript, and the other sites were requested over HTTP. Every address answered. A site that blocks scripted requests can answer with an error while the page opens in a browser. One topic that exists only in release 2608.00 of the SAP S/4HANA Cloud Public Edition What's New text was confirmed with that release number.

== Sources by group

=== General

Sources for the general SAP pages: landscape, architecture, integration, SAP BTP, CAP, SAPUI5 and SAP Fiori, extensibility and operations. It also holds every source that pages of more than one area cite.

== Non-official and sign-in sources

=== Non-official sources

Third-party pages. A blog or a competitor's product page is cited only to show how others describe something, never as the authority for a rule.

* https://blog.adesso-bc.com/en/checking-an-odata-v4-service-on-the-sap-gateway-sap-integration-pt-3/[adesso: Checking an OData V4 Service on the SAP Gateway] -- cited in xref:erp/sap/odata-and-sap-gateway.adoc[OData and SAP Gateway]
* https://frappe.io/erpnext[ERPNext, Frappe] -- cited in xref:erp/index.adoc[ERP] and 1 more page
* https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/dynamics-365[Microsoft Dynamics 365] -- cited in xref:erp/index.adoc[ERP] and 1 more page
* https://www.odoo.com/[Odoo] -- cited in xref:erp/index.adoc[ERP] and 1 more page

* https://www.oracle.com/erp/what-is-erp/[What Is ERP?, Oracle] -- cited in xref:erp/index.adoc[ERP] and 1 more page

=== Sources whose content needs a sign-in

These addresses are public start pages, but part of what they lead to, such as downloads, API definitions or support content, needs an SAP sign-in. No account was used for this site, so those parts were not read.

* https://api.sap.com[api.sap.com] -- cited in xref:erp/sap/integration-suite-and-business-accelerator-hub.adoc[SAP Integration Suite and Business Accelerator Hub]
